### 11.21.6.3 PM-T02 Investment Automation SKU invocation (valid set, pairwise)

| Parameter | Values | Count |
|---|---|---|
| `sku` | INV-SVC-01 … INV-SVC-24 | 24 |

| `investor_jurisdiction` | IN · US · EU · UK | 4 |
| `locale` | en-IN · hi-IN · en-GB · de-DE · fr-FR · ar-AE | 6 |

**Method:** Pairwise (2-way), valid values only. Raw product 16,588,800 · valid not enumerated (product too large; feasibility by AC-3 + search) · required t-tuples 1,677 · **180 cases** (TC-V12-T-905…TC-V12-T-1084) · verification: 0 missing tuples, 0 invalid rows, 279 tuples excluded by constraint.

#### Group TC-V12-T-M02: Pairwise combinatorial cases – Investment Automation SKU invocation (valid set)

**Common for TC-V12-T-905…TC-V12-T-1084:** Pre: PP-01 per zone; Investment Automation tenant "Lumen Robotics Pvt Ltd" (IN) or "Lumen Robotics GmbH" (EU) on the INV plan; securities packs of the row jurisdiction Approved (synthetic approval in test, counsel-signed in production); investor fixtures with KYC state per row. Steps: 1. Authenticate as the row role on the row channel. 2. Run the SKU's first workflow with FX-INV-<sku>-v1. 3. Read rendered content, outbox, data-room access log, audit and simulator logs in the zone. Links for every row: FR-V12-N / FR-V12-O (Parts 11.16a–e); INV-SVC-01…24; RP-IN-SEC, RP-US-REGD, RP-US-REGCF, RP-EU-PROSP, RP-UK-FINPROM, RP-AML-KYC, RP-INV-ADVICE; DEC-783. Generated by `assembly/v12b/t_04_models.py` (model PM-T02); IDs assigned by `t_05_build.py`.

### 11.21.6.5 PM-T03 Consequential-effect gateway

**Purpose.** One dispatcher, one PDP and one consumption ledger decide every external effect of the platform (Part 11.17a). PM-T03 tests that decision for **15 effect types** across all money-, law- and safety-critical capabilities under the same control values, so that a fix in one capability cannot silently weaken another.

**Effects and their allow outcomes.**

| Effect | Workflow | Mandate type | Personal recipient | ALLOW outcome (oracle) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| E01 email outreach send | SM-SVC-06-WF02 | MT-OUT | Yes | Email sent once via the zone SES identity; outbox row; Delivered by signed webhook |
| E02 WhatsApp template send | SM-SVC-07-WF02 | MT-OUT | Yes | Approved template sent once via WhatsApp Cloud API; signed delivery receipt |
| E03 SMS/RCS send | SM-SVC-38-WF02 | none (per-item approval only) | Yes | DLT-registered template sent once via the aggregator; DLR stored |
| E04 outbound AI voice call | SM-SVC-26-WF02 | none (per-item approval only) | Yes | Call placed; AI identifies itself as AI at the start; recording only per pack; human hand-off offered |
| E05 content/page publication | SM-SVC-11-WF03 | MT-PUB | No | Approved content version published (atomic pointer swap, hash-bound) |
| E06 ad budget change | SM-SVC-14-WF02 | MT-SPEND | No | Budget change applied once on the ad account; spend ledger updated |
| E07 quote issue within discount band | SM-SVC-17-WF02 | MT-COM | No | Quote issued at the approved price-book revision; discount ≤ band |
| E08 purchase order to supplier | SM-SVC-39-WF03 | MT-PROC | No | PO committed to the approved supplier; payable commitment posted |
| E09 supplier pay-out via PSP | SM-SVC-39-WF03 | MT-PAY | No | Pay-out instruction to the licensed PSP with idempotency key; Dr Payable / Cr PSP clearing; daily reconciliation match |
| E10 receivable collection via PSP mandate | SM-SVC-32-WF04 | MT-PAY | No | Collection through the registered PSP mandate after pre-debit notice; receivable ledger updated; reconciliation match |
| E11 AI-to-AI order acceptance | SM-SVC-28-WF03 | MT-COM | No | Order accepted from a verified counterparty agent within negotiation bounds; signed acceptance; commerce sub-ledger posting |
| E12 automatic bid submission | SM-SVC-19-WF03 | MT-BID | No | Exact approved package hash submitted to the portal; submission receipt stored; mandate count 1 → Exhausted |
| E13 price experiment exposure | SM-SVC-37-WF03 | MT-PRICE | No | Exposure switched between the two approved price-book revisions within the exposure cap |
| E14 investor outreach message | INV-SVC-04-WF02 | MT-OUT (INV) | Yes | Investor message sent once to a verified recipient; no offer terms unless eligible |
| E15 formal securities offer issue | INV-SVC-11-WF02 | none (per-item approval only) | Yes | Offer document issued to the named eligible investor after two-person approval; legends per pack |

**Oracle decision order** (implements §11.17.5.12 and the capability rules): 1 kill switch → 2 jurisdiction enabled → 3 pack Approved → 4 effect has a mandate path for L3/L4 → 5 level ≤ the register maximum → 6 approval or mandate state → 7 consent (personal-recipient effects only; registry scrub for SMS and voice) → 8 data class (Restricted never in an external payload; payments carry tokens only; personal data in public content needs publication consent) → 9 bid package/price equals the approved hash → 10 per-action cap → 11 aggregate cap → ALLOW. Caps are inclusive (ASSUMPTION-1121). For L1/L2 a value above the approver's threshold holds for a higher approver instead of blocking (DEC-038).

#### Layer L1: full space (variable strength)

| Parameter | Values | Count |
|---|---|---|
| `effect` | E01 email outreach send · E02 WhatsApp template send · E03 SMS/RCS send · E04 outbound AI voice call · E05 content/page publication · E06 ad budget change · E07 quote issue within discount band · E08 purchase order to supplier · E09 supplier pay-out via PSP · E10 receivable collection via PSP mandate · E11 AI-to-AI order acceptance · E12 automatic bid submission · E13 price experiment exposure · E14 investor outreach message · E15 formal securities offer issue | 15 |
| `authorization` | L1 human dispatch · L2 approved hash valid · L3 mandate Active · L4 step in approved plan graph · L2 payload changed · L2 approval expired · L3 mandate Suspended · L3 mandate Revoked · L3 mandate Exhausted · L4 step outside plan graph | 10 |
| `value` | below per-action cap · exactly at cap · above per-action cap · crosses aggregate cap | 4 |
| `pack` | IN pack Approved · EU pack Approved · pack Draft · jurisdiction not enabled | 4 |
| `region` | IN zone · EU zone | 2 |
| `kill_switch` | off · tenant scope · platform scope | 3 |
| `consent` | valid basis · withdrawn · unknown · registry/DND-listed | 4 |
| `data` | Public/Internal · Personal · Restricted | 3 |

**Method:** Variable strength: 3-way among the 7 control parameters + 2-way effect × each control. Raw product 172,800 · valid 172,800 · required t-tuples 2,818 · **200 cases** (TC-V12-T-1138…TC-V12-T-1337) · pairwise alone would need 151 · verification: 0 missing tuples, 0 invalid rows. Outcome mix: ALLOW 2 · HOLD 4 · BLOCK 194.

#### Group TC-V12-T-M03: Variable-strength combinatorial cases – consequential-effect gateway (full space)

**Common for TC-V12-T-1138…TC-V12-T-1337:** Pre: PP-01 in the row zone; mandate, approval, pack, kill-switch, consent and data-class fixtures seeded per row through the test-only seeding API (never in production); provider simulators for SES, WhatsApp, DLT aggregator, CPaaS, CMS, ad platform, PSP (UPI/e-NACH in IN, SEPA in EU), counterparty agent and tender portal. Steps: 1. Seed the row state. 2. Release the effect step of the named workflow through the dispatcher. 3. Read the PDP decision log (rule order of §11.17.5.12), outbox, consumption ledger, provider simulator log, reconciliation queue and audit in the zone. Links for every row: FR-V12-P-001…069 (mandate engine), FR-V12-P-070…099 (commerce and payments), FR-V12-P bids (Part 11.17b), FR-V12-Q (voice), FR-V12-N/O (investor); §11.17.5.12 PDP order; DEC-779…783. Generated by `assembly/v12b/t_04_models.py` (model PM-T03); IDs assigned by `t_05_build.py`.

#### Layer L3: single fault × effect (exhaustive pairing)

Each of the 16 invalid control values is applied to each of the 15 effects with every other control valid. ALLOW rows in this layer are expected where the faulted control does not apply to the effect (for example consent on a supplier pay-out, or Restricted bank details that travel only as PSP tokens); the expected text states why.

**Method:** Single-fault: each of the 16 invalid control values × each of the 15 effects (exhaustive pairing), other controls valid. **240 cases** (TC-V12-T-1482…TC-V12-T-1721) · verification: 0 missing tuples, 0 invalid rows. Outcome mix: ALLOW 29 · HOLD 34 · BLOCK 177.

#### Group TC-V12-T-M03N: Single-fault cases – consequential-effect gateway

**Common for TC-V12-T-1482…TC-V12-T-1721:** Pre: PP-01 in the row zone; mandate, approval, pack, kill-switch, consent and data-class fixtures seeded per row through the test-only seeding API (never in production); provider simulators for SES, WhatsApp, DLT aggregator, CPaaS, CMS, ad platform, PSP (UPI/e-NACH in IN, SEPA in EU), counterparty agent and tender portal. Steps: 1. Seed the row state. 2. Release the effect step of the named workflow through the dispatcher. 3. Read the PDP decision log (rule order of §11.17.5.12), outbox, consumption ledger, provider simulator log, reconciliation queue and audit in the zone. Links for every row: FR-V12-P-001…069 (mandate engine), FR-V12-P-070…099 (commerce and payments), FR-V12-P bids (Part 11.17b), FR-V12-Q (voice), FR-V12-N/O (investor); §11.17.5.12 PDP order; DEC-779…783. #### Group TC-V12-T-M04: 3-way combinatorial cases – data residency

**Common for TC-V12-T-1722…TC-V12-T-1863:** Pre: both zone stacks (or the EU-configured test cell) with tenants "Kestrel Analytics" (IN) and "Nordlicht GmbH" (EU); residency probe AU-296/CJ-TC-09 scanner with read access to every store in both zones; operator account with JIT workflow. Steps: 1. Perform the row operation on the row component as the row actor. 2. Run the residency probe across all stores of the other zone (DB, buckets, cache keys, index, logs, SIEM, backups). 3. Inspect provider/region metadata and the audit trail. Links for every row: DEC-772, DEC-776, DEC-786, DEC-787; CJ-TC-09 (both directions); FR-V12-R-056…095 (Vercel path, recovery); ENT-DAT-02/03. | Parameter | Values | Count |
|---|---|---|
| `zone` | IN · EU | 2 |
| `failure` | AZ loss (API/workers) · queue/cache (Valkey) loss · worker crash mid-dispatch · Vercel function region outage · Supabase project unavailable · model provider outage · PSP outage or timeout · CPaaS (telephony) outage · email/SMS provider outage · KMS key unavailable | 10 |
| `in_flight` | idle · MT-OUT dispatch batch · MT-PAY pay-out · MT-BID submission 30 min before deadline · live AI voice call · investor closing allotment (INV-SVC-13) | 6 |
| `load` | normal · peak (LT-04/LT-11) | 2 |

**Method:** 3-way. Raw product 240 · valid 240 · required t-tuples 304 · **120 cases** (TC-V12-T-2004…TC-V12-T-2123) · pairwise alone would need 60 · verification: 0 missing tuples, 0 invalid rows.

#### Group TC-V12-T-M09: 3-way combinatorial cases – resilience with work in flight

**Common for TC-V12-T-2004…TC-V12-T-2123:** Pre: `staging` or `perf` per zone with fault injection (chaos scripts, provider simulators in outage mode, KMS deny policy); in-flight fixture per row started 60 s before the fault. Steps: 1. Start the in-flight work. 2. Inject the failure. 3. Observe detection, paging, containment and recovery. 4. Reconcile effects and compute achieved RPO/RTO. Links for every row: DEC-771 (RTO ≤ 120 min), DEC-787 (RPO ≤ 15 min), DEC-773 (24×7 on-call); NFR-005/007/012/026; DR-1…8 (Part 6.1) and DR-V12-T-01…08 (§11.21.3.7); Part 11.19b DR-R1…R8. #### XT-12 Messaging window (DEC-777)

The platform window is the half-open interval [09:00:00, 21:00:00) in the recipient's local time; a stricter pack wins; transactional messages follow their own rules (DEC-931).

| `pack` | platform default · stricter pack (weekdays 10:00–20:00, no Sun/holiday) | 2 |
| `message` | promotional · transactional | 2 |
